    ✓ FORGERY: a row pinning an attacker key with a genuinely valid signature is NOT valid (317 ms)
    ✓ CURRENT key: a row pinning the key this box holds verifies (561 ms)

      ✓ MISSING key pair → refuses to boot and mints NOTHING (39 ms)

      ✓ MISSING key pair → generates AND logs a loud dev-key warning (159 ms)
      ✓ UNUSABLE key pair → refuses rather than overwriting the key that signed old certificates (128 ms)

      ✓ matching fingerprint → boots (114 ms)
      ✓ mismatched fingerprint → refuses to boot even though the key loads fine (125 ms)
      ✓ mismatch is fatal in dev too — a generated dev key can never match a pinned fingerprint (100 ms)
      ✓ fingerprintPublicKey is stable, hex, and tolerant of CRLF checkout (247 ms)

    ✓ health: the 5th wrong current password locks the account for 15 minutes (41 ms)
    ✓ provider: while locked, even the right current password changes nothing (6 ms)
    ✓ health: while locked, even the right current password changes nothing (10 ms)
    ✓ provider: an expired lock clears itself and a right change resets the count (12 ms)
    ✓ health: an expired lock clears itself and a right change resets the count (12 ms)
    ✓ provider: a failed attempt is audited as FAILURE, with no password material (12 ms)
    ✓ health: a failed attempt is audited as FAILURE, with no password material (10 ms)
    ✓ the lock that locks the change door also locks sign-in (one counter, not two) (54 ms)

    ✓ mounted on /auth/provider/change-password inside the prefix loop (2 ms)
